#### P3-C baseline STOP and executable source remediation

The P3-C candidate is a fail-closed STOP, not baseline input. The checksum-bound discovery input is `qmx-p3-source-remediation-design.md` SHA-256 `c0dcddff7ad65b57461ab7926d253579510a846ed29105fd52b78677a2d44103`; the exact behavior/suite map is `qmx-p3-remediation-test-map.tsv` SHA-256 `b66a71591204bc2f07f0db0ce6f9d3929373b6e18b158bb91c20687d30164f16`; the 199-row structural forensic is `qmx-p3c-baseline-structural-forensic.tsv` SHA-256 `858d944201caaf444993fe8079e9ef46e9aa8b4d46b9d72121ccff30ab566b8a`. The reviewed corrections in this section supersede only the discovery boolean/result-construction shape, CheckCommand property count, DGI raw/consumer explanation and strengthen-ID omissions in the first artifact; its remaining evidence and arithmetic stay inputs. The remediation adds six declarations/files to the verified P3-A state: **715 declarations / 713 files, 235 public contracts / 775 used consumer entries**. Owners remain 37, seams 11, internal/total layers 48/49 and grants/coarse pairs 66/12. Declared allow edges are remeasured from the materialized candidate; no previous count is carried forward.

The singular source mechanisms are:

1. **Run discovery coordination.** Add internal `Analysis\Run\Discovery\AnalysisFileDiscovery`, `DiscoveredAnalysisFiles` and string-backed enum `GeneratedFilePolicy` with exact cases `Include` and `Exclude`. `AnalysisFileDiscovery::__construct(FileDiscoveryInterface $defaultDiscovery, GeneratedFileFilterInterface $generatedFileFilter)` and `discover(list<AbsolutePath> $paths, AbsolutePath $projectRoot, GeneratedFilePolicy $generatedFilePolicy, ?FileDiscoveryInterface $override = null): DiscoveredAnalysisFiles`; there is no boolean policy parameter. The coordinator owns deduplication and filtering. `DiscoveredAnalysisFiles` has a private constructor and an internal `fromDiscovery(list<SplFileInfo> $eligibleFiles, list<RelativePath> $generatedExcludedFiles, int $discoveredCount): self` factory used only by `AnalysisFileDiscovery`; its lists/count are factory-trusted outputs, not a separately advertised public invariant. A reverse-grep enforces that sole construction route, so no extra invariant test ID is added. `AnalysisPipeline` replaces its default-discovery/filter dependencies with the coordinator, converts transitional configuration once to the enum, deletes `discoverAnalysisFiles()`, and goes from twelve to eleven constructor parameters. `AnalysisConfigurator` registers only literal internal ID `qmx.analysis.run.file_discovery` with default discovery and generated filter; the enum/result need no service registration, alias, consumer or grant.
2. **Console scope resolution.** Add internal `Infrastructure\Console\CheckScopeResolver::__construct(GitScopeResolver, ScopeWarningChecker)` with `resolve(InputInterface, TransitionalResolvedConfiguration): ResolvedCheckScope`, and internal readonly `ResolvedCheckScope(GitScopeResolution, list<string> warnings)`. Resolution preserves git-reference failure ordering, then computes warnings without writing output. `CheckCommand` removes its dead logger and both leaf dependencies, injects the facade, and goes from twelve to ten constructor dependencies and fifteen to thirteen properties. `OutputConfigurator` registers/injects the literal facade; no contract, alias, consumer or grant is added.
3. **Measurement profiling.** `MeasurementAggregationService` imports the public `Core\Profiler\ProfilerInterface`, not internal `NullProfiler`. In `aggregate()`, resolve `$profiler = $this->profilerHolder?->get()` exactly once. Private static `start(?ProfilerInterface $profiler, string $name): void` calls `$profiler?->start($name, 'pipeline')`; private static `stop(?ProfilerInterface $profiler, string $name): void` calls `$profiler?->stop($name)`. Aggregation and global start/stop events remain unconditional and ordered exactly as today, including an empty collector list; the sorted-collector debug event remains conditional on `sortedCollectors !== []`, and reaggregation remains conditional on `globalDefinitions !== []`. The injected-null path stays allocation-free and silent, while a holder observes the same span/log order. The existing `MeasurementAggregationServiceTest::itHandlesEmptyCollectorList` is strengthened to assert global start/stop as well as aggregation start/stop; no ID/count is added. The manifest adds a real permanent `Analysis.Evidence.Measurement` import consumer to `ProfilerInterface`; this is a contract edge, not a grant, seam or new layer. The candidate NPath 640 and CCN 11 must disappear or improve below configured boundaries, never enter the baseline.
4. **Successful collection payload.** Add public Run contract `Analysis\Run\Contract\Collection\SuccessfulFileProcessing` with constructor `(MetricBag $fileBag, list<CallableWithMetrics> $callableMetrics = [], array<string,array{subject:MetricSubject,metrics:MetricBag,line:int}> $classMetrics = [], array<string,array{symbolPath:SymbolPath,metrics:MetricBag,line:int}> $namespaceMetrics = [], list<Dependency> $dependencies = [], list<Suppression> $suppressions = [], list<ThresholdOverride> $thresholdOverrides = [], list<ThresholdDiagnostic> $thresholdDiagnostics = [])`. `FileProcessingResult` becomes a four-field terminal union `(RelativePath, ?SuccessfulFileProcessing, ?FileProcessingFailureKind, ?string)`; `success(RelativePath, SuccessfulFileProcessing)` and the existing failure factory enforce exactly one complete terminal state. Getters delegate to the payload. `Infrastructure.Parallel` does not import the nested payload: the exact manifest relation is `contract_composition` with target `SuccessfulFileProcessing`, carrier `Analysis\Run\Contract\Collection\FileProcessingResult`, boundary `Infrastructure\Parallel\FileProcessingTask` and owner `Infrastructure.Parallel`; the separately used normal `FileProcessingResult` consumer remains the import authorization. `CollectionOrchestrator`, `FileProcessingTask`, `AmphpParallelStrategy` and `CollectionPhaseOutput` retain the outer result and require zero payload import. PHP/igbinary round trips plus the real Amp worker remain the wire oracle.
5. **Narrow qmx policy.** Remove the unused `AnalysisPipeline` import from `DependencyGraphInterface`; it is a non-edge and raw CBO remains 27. Set the existing point threshold to 28 with inclusive no-headroom semantics: the current 27 passes and the next edge at 28 fails. Its reason names the exact five net additions `DependencyGraphBuilderInterface`, `DependencyGraphBuilder`, `AnalysisPipeline`, `DependencyGraphProjector` and `MeasurementAggregationInterface`; no namespace exclusion or baseline row is added. Add point CBO thresholds with one-edge headroom only to `AbstractCollector` 27, `AggregationStrategy` 38, `MetricBag` 67, `MetricDefinition` 33, `MetricName` 63, `MetricRepositoryInterface` 46, `ResettableVisitorInterface` 23 and `SymbolLevel` 34. `MetricBag` raw CBO is 66 after the `SuccessfulFileProcessing` edge. Project-size-scaled ClassRank point thresholds are `warning=0.035 error=0.035` for `MetricBag` (effective about 0.01309 above observed raw 0.0128228) and `warning=0.020 error=0.020` for `MetricRepositoryInterface` (effective about 0.00748 above observed raw 0.00745523). Rekey the exact AbstractCollector NOC path; remove its stale old instability path if the effect is zero; add the exact `CollectorRuntimeConfigurationStore` data-class store exclusion. There is no Measurement or DependencyModel namespace blanket. Retain at most the single independently reviewed `ns:Qualimetrix\Analysis\Evidence\Measurement\Contract` CBO 49 baseline row, and only if a fresh post-fix candidate still reports it.

P3-R1 is the sole atomic source/test/governance-source writer. Its production set is exactly `src/Analysis/Run/Discovery/{AnalysisFileDiscovery,DiscoveredAnalysisFiles,GeneratedFilePolicy}.php`, `src/Analysis/Run/Pipeline/AnalysisPipeline.php`, `src/Infrastructure/Console/{CheckScopeResolver,ResolvedCheckScope}.php`, `src/Infrastructure/Console/Command/CheckCommand.php`, `src/Analysis/Evidence/Measurement/Aggregation/MeasurementAggregationService.php`, `src/Analysis/Run/Contract/Collection/{SuccessfulFileProcessing,FileProcessingResult}.php`, `src/Analysis/Run/Collection/FileProcessor.php`, `src/Infrastructure/DependencyInjection/Configurator/AnalysisConfigurator.php`, `src/Infrastructure/DependencyInjection/Configurator/OutputConfigurator.php`, and these nine annotation/source files: `src/Analysis/Evidence/Measurement/Contract/{AbstractCollector,AggregationStrategy,MetricBag,MetricDefinition,MetricName,MetricRepositoryInterface,ResettableVisitorInterface,SymbolLevel}.php` plus `src/Analysis/Evidence/DependencyModel/Contract/DependencyGraphInterface.php`. `MeasurementConfigurator.php` is a reverse-grep/compiled-container verification target and is not a writer when no semantic change is required. The finite governance-source set is exactly `docs/internal/modular-architecture-manifest.json`, `docs/internal/modular-architecture-manifest.schema.json`, `scripts/generate-modular-architecture-production-inventory.php`, `scripts/generate-modular-architecture-test-inventory.php`, `tests/Architecture/Integration/ModularArchitectureGovernanceIntegrationTest.php` and `tests/Integration/Architecture/DogfoodingTopologyTest.php`. The schema change adds only the optional, mutually exclusive `contract_composition` shape above; it does not weaken import/grant validation. #### P3-R3 parallel task-construction remediation

**Status:** Completed and published. The pre-fix candidate exposed two source regressions rather than baseline work: `StrategySelector::__construct` grew from eight to ten parameters, and `AmphpParallelStrategy` grew from `Ca=2, Ce=11, I=0.846154` to `Ca=2, Ce=13, I=0.866667`. P3-R3 moved task-construction metadata to the factory below; the published no-cache candidate has no selector overinjection finding and restores `AmphpParallelStrategy` to `Ca=2, Ce=11, I=0.846154`. No qmx exclusion or new baseline identity accepts either regression.

`Infrastructure\Parallel\FileProcessingTaskFactory` is a subject-cohesive internal collaborator: it owns construction of the existing serializable `FileProcessingTask`, not a generic factory role or a new public port. Its exact planned surface is:

```text
__construct(
    CollectorRuntimeConfigurationStoreInterface $collectorRuntimeConfigurationStore,
    class-string<DependencyTraversalParticipantInterface> $dependencyTraversalParticipantClass,
    list<class-string<MetricCollectorInterface>> $collectorClasses = [],
    list<class-string<DerivedCollectorInterface>> $derivedCollectorClasses = [],
    list<class-string<RuleInterface>> $ruleClasses = [],
)

create(AbsolutePath $filePath, AbsolutePath $projectRoot, ?AbsolutePath $cacheDir): FileProcessingTask

hasCollectors(): bool
```

The factory **does take both** the traversal implementation class-string and the runtime-configuration store. It validates the class-string against `DependencyTraversalParticipantInterface` at construction, while `WorkerBootstrap` retains its worker-side validation as the serialized-boundary defence. `create()` calls `CollectorRuntimeConfigurationStoreInterface::current()` for every task and copies that value's normalized payload into the task; it must not snapshot the store value in the factory constructor or at container compilation. This preserves the current late runtime configuration installed after container compilation and before collection. Collector, derived-collector and rule class lists are immutable compile-time metadata on the factory. `hasCollectors()` is the factory's only readiness query and returns whether the base-collector list is non-empty; no collector-list getter, traversal-class getter, runtime-store getter or generic metadata accessor is added.

`AmphpParallelStrategy::__construct(FileProcessingTaskFactory $fileProcessingTaskFactory, LoggerInterface $logger = new NullLogger())` receives the factory and asks it to create each submitted task. Remove its collector, derived-collector, rule, collector-config and traversal-class fields/setters and its direct imports of `FileProcessingTask`, `CollectorRuntimeConfiguration` and `DependencyTraversalParticipantInterface`. `execute()` preserves the current fallback order before worker-pool construction: rejected parallelization/empty input, minimum-file threshold, missing project root, `!$fileProcessingTaskFactory->hasCollectors()`, and unavailable Amp transport all execute the supplied sequential processor. Only after all five gates pass may `executeParallel()` construct the pool and call `create()` for each task. The missing-collector warning and fallback remain observable; readiness is not inferred by creating a task. `StrategySelector` retains only strategy selection and per-run worker/project/cache configuration; its constructor becomes the five dependencies `AmphpParallelStrategy`, `SequentialStrategy`, `TransitionalRuntimeConfigurationProviderInterface`, `WorkerCountDetector` and `LoggerInterface`, and `select()` no longer transfers collector or traversal metadata. This lowers the selector below its historical eight-parameter baseline ceiling and restores the strategy to `Ce=11` without moving legitimate edges or hiding them.

DI wiring is explicit and keeps the late boundary intact. `CollectorConfigurator` imports and registers `FileProcessingTaskFactory`, injects it into `AmphpParallelStrategy`, and leaves its named arguments for the later DependencyModel configurator/compiler pass. `DependencyModelConfigurator` retains selector registration/aliasing and supplies the factory's traversal class-string plus `CollectorRuntimeConfigurationStoreInterface` reference by the exact string service ID `Qualimetrix\Infrastructure\Parallel\FileProcessingTaskFactory`; it must not import the internal factory declaration or add a second grant. `ParallelCollectorClassesCompilerPass` imports and targets the factory definition and writes its three named list arguments. Configurator ordering is already `CollectorConfigurator -> MeasurementConfigurator -> DependencyModelConfigurator`, so the definition and store alias exist before the final factory arguments are bound. Neither configurator nor the compiler pass may instantiate the factory or read `current()`; the compiler pass no longer targets `StrategySelector`. No service locator, mutable metadata setter or new contract is introduced.

#### P3-R4 final-review remediation

**Status:** Completed and republished; final independent re-review is GO with no findings. Final review found six issues after the P3-C checkpoint: two constructor regressions caused by misplaced orchestration (finding 1), inconsistent main/worker collector-configuration validation (finding 2), a false-positive `contract_composition` containment predicate (finding 3), an incomplete terminal-state invariant (finding 4), stale current-architecture documentation (finding 5), and stale plan status/count claims (finding 6). P3-R4 closes only those findings; it introduces no public contract, compatibility shim, qmx exclusion or accepted debt.

Finding 1 is split by subject ownership. `Analysis\Evidence\Measurement\Runtime\CollectorRuntimeConfigurationStore` owns applying the configuration it stores: its constructor receives `iterable<CollectorRuntimeConfigurableInterface> $collectors = []`, where the empty default preserves the three existing no-argument test/integration construction sites; production DI always supplies the tagged iterable. `replace()` stores the exact supplied object and passes that same object to every collector, and `reset()` delegates to `replace(CollectorRuntimeConfiguration::empty())`. `MeasurementConfigurator` supplies `TaggedIteratorArgument('qmx.measurement.runtime_configurable_collector')`. `AnalysisRuntimeConfigurator` neither imports nor iterates configurable collectors and no longer owns cache reset; its exact constructor returns to seven dependencies: `TransitionalRuntimeConfigurationProviderInterface`, `RuleOptionsRegistry`, `RuleRegistryInterface`, `ComputedMetricsConfigResolver`, `FrameworkNamespacesHolder`, `iterable<AnalysisLifecycleHookInterface>`, and `CollectorRuntimeConfigurationStoreInterface`. `RuntimeConfigurator` receives `CacheFactory` as its seventh constructor dependency and starts every `configure()` with `CacheFactory::reset()` immediately followed by `AnalysisRuntimeConfigurator::resetRunState()`, both before logger setup, deferred-warning replay or application of new run state. `OutputConfigurator` removes the cache and tagged collector arguments from `AnalysisRuntimeConfigurator` and injects `CacheFactory` into the outer configurator. This removes exactly the two current `AnalysisRuntimeConfigurator::__construct` baseline rows (constructor-overinjection and parameter-count, both magnitude 9) without hiding either finding.

The debug adapter's collection/application work moves to a single internal `Infrastructure\Console\LayerAssignmentResolver`, not to a new capability port or role bucket. Its constructor has exactly six dependencies: `CollectionOrchestratorInterface`, `DependencyGraphBuilderInterface`, `ArchitectureProcessorInterface`, `MetricRepositoryFactoryInterface`, `FileDiscoveryFactoryInterface`, and `GeneratedFileFilterInterface`. Configuration remains owned by `LayerAssignmentCommand`: after runtime setup it projects the exact `paths`, `pathExcludes` and `projectRoot` values into `resolve(...)` or `resolveIncludingGenerated(...)`, selected from `includeGenerated`. The resolver therefore owns discovery, optional generated-file filtering, collection, graph/class-set preparation and `resolveAll()` without importing `TransitionalResolvedConfiguration` or accepting a boolean policy flag. `LayerAssignmentCommand` retains only CLI validation, configuration/runtime setup, that explicit projection, error mapping and report rendering, with exactly three constructor dependencies: `ConfigurationPipelineInterface`, `RuntimeConfigurator`, and `LayerAssignmentResolver`. `ArchitectureConfigurator` registers the resolver by literal internal class/service ID, supplies its six public contract references, and injects it into the command without publishing a new alias or adding an internal grant. This adds one declaration/file and removes exactly the two current `LayerAssignmentCommand::__construct` baseline rows (constructor-overinjection and parameter-count, both magnitude 8). The split API also removes the accidental resolver-to-Configuration import: a fresh no-baseline measurement restores `Analysis\Configuration` distance to exactly `0.5555555555555556`, equal to the retained `0.555556` ceiling, and the baseline-backed selfcheck has zero violations.

Finding 2 is closed at the value object's sole construction boundary. `CollectorRuntimeConfiguration::__construct()` validates that `lcomExcludedMethods` is a list of strings and performs the existing stable de-duplication; `fromPayload()` validates only the payload envelope/known key and delegates the list to the constructor. Direct main-process construction and worker `fromPayload()` therefore reject the same malformed `['ok', 42]` value with `InvalidArgumentException`, while valid payload and wire format remain unchanged. Add exactly one test ID, `CollectorRuntimeConfigurationTest::itRejectsMalformedExcludedMethodsAtConstruction`; no data provider or additional case expands discovery.

Finding 3 is a fail-closed AST correction, not a manifest-schema relaxation. Rename the generator's computed evidence field to `contract_property_containments` and populate it only from declared typed properties plus typed promoted constructor properties. A normal method or constructor parameter, regardless of visibility, is transient input and never proves carrier containment. The `contract_composition` validator resolves the exact native target only against this property set. Strengthen the existing `ModularArchitectureGovernanceIntegrationTest::itEncodesThePostP3RunConfigurationMeasurementAndDependencyModelBoundaries` with a source-backed negative: write a synthetic carrier PHP fixture/source in the test's isolated tree whose only native mention of the target is an ordinary, non-promoted method parameter and which declares no target-typed property; add the corresponding composition row to that isolated manifest candidate; then invoke the real production inventory extractor/validator and require rejection for missing stored containment. Mutating a precomputed declaration row or hand-building the extractor's containment evidence is forbidden because it would not exercise the defective AST predicate. Retain the positive promoted-private `FileProcessingResult -> SuccessfulFileProcessing` case and all existing PHPDoc/direct-import negatives. This adds no tracked fixture, manifest relation, schema field, allow edge or test ID.

Finding 4 strengthens the existing `FileProcessingResult` constructor invariant. Acceptance requires exactly one complete terminal: either a non-null success with both failure fields null, or null success with both failure kind and error non-null. Every empty, partial-failure, partial-dual and complete-dual tuple throws `LogicException`. Expand only `FileProcessingResultTest::itRejectsPartialOrDualTerminalStates` to invoke the private constructor reflectively for the exhaustive invalid tuples; success/failure factories and the serialized wire shape remain unchanged, and no test ID is added.

Findings 5-6 are factual publication work. `AGENTS.md`, `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`, and `docs/adr/0022-capability-oriented-modular-monolith.md` must describe the landed snapshot rather than 709/707, 66 grants or a future P3-C publication. This plan must distinguish the completed P3-C checkpoint from the planned P3-R4/final republication and use the reviewed 498/7,223 starting inventory. After source/governance publication, all four documents state the measured final target: **717 declarations / 715 files, 235 contracts / 775 used consumer entries, 67 exact grants / 12 coarse grant pairs, 37 owners, 11 seams, 48/49 layers, 266 declared allows, and 498 PHPUnit classes / 7,224 cases**. `src/Analysis/Evidence/Measurement/README.md` records that the store applies replacement/reset values to configurable collectors; `src/Infrastructure/Console/README.md` is the canonical Console tree/runtime-ownership document and adds `LayerAssignmentResolver`; `src/Infrastructure/README.md` updates the higher-level runtime-composition summary. Counts are asserted from generated artifacts; prose never substitutes for generation.

### P4 — Isolate Architecture policy and its circular-dependency preparation

Files: current `src/Architecture/**`; cycle detector/result files assigned by P0; architecture DI configurator; related commands/adapters; central configuration/pipeline seams; qmx rules; focused tests/READMEs.

Entry gate: all P3 remediation, resumed P3-C publication, baseline reconciliation and final review gates are green. P4 is blocked/not started until then. P4 must not assume a P3 graph or lifecycle participant port; it introduces Architecture's own directed configuration and graph-preparation boundary only after proving its consumers.

- Keep declared layer configuration, expansion, membership and layer-violation evaluation under `Analysis\Policy\Architecture`.
- Apply the P4 manifest rows and explicit consumers, regenerate qmx/inventories, and remove the `ArchitectureLifecycleHook`, `ConfigLoadException` and deferred-warning seams only after returning each declaration to its true owner leaves the projected graph acyclic.
- Move `CircularDependencyDetector`, `Cycle`, their result model/options/rule and prepared state under Architecture-owned circular-dependency preparation in `Analysis\Policy\Architecture`; consume only `DependencyModel\Contract`. P3 does not assign either current declaration to DependencyModel or a speculative peer evidence owner.
- Define the minimum Architecture contract required by debug adapters. Run gets no generic P3 lifecycle/graph port; P4 defines a narrow directed boundary only from its proven class-universe/configuration input. Define a CircularDependency contract only if a named consumer remains after removing its universal-context payload.
- Do not consume a speculative Analysis\Run participant port. If P4 needs inversion, define the minimum consumer-owned port from the proven graph/class-universe input and test it in P4.
- Remove `ResolvedConfiguration::$architecture`, direct `ArchitectureConfigurationFactory` construction in central configuration, direct Architecture internals from Run, and cycles from `AnalysisContext`.
- Each capability parses its node from `ConfigurationDocumentInterface`; central Configuration stores neither feature object nor a heterogeneous resolved-object bag.

DoD: no `configuration -> architecture`, `run -> architecture-internal`, `run -> circular-dependency-internal`, or mutual allow; external imports target only proven contracts; layer-policy, circular-dependency and debug-command behaviour remains; two sequential runs prove both capability states reset independently.

Health consumes the transitional `excludeHealth` field through a distinct Health-owned `HealthConfiguration` data contract; that parsed configuration is not an alias for the formula-exclusion operation port.
- Dissolve P3's `TransitionalMetricEnricher` and the remaining `TransitionalEnrichmentResult` shell after P4 has removed cycles: Measurement owns aggregation/global reaggregation, ComputedMetrics owns computed evaluation, and Run keeps invocation order only.

- Keep Health as a named subdomain for this package; split only if inventory shows an independent lifecycle/public consumer set.
- Move health computation identified by P0 out of Reporting; pure health rendering remains in Reporting and consumes a narrow ComputedMetrics/Health read contract.

DoD: `Analysis\Configuration`, `Analysis\Run` and `RuntimeConfigurator` contain no computed-feature knowledge; two different configurations in one process cannot leak definitions; formula cycles/errors, exclude-health and all health formats retain direct regressions.

### P7 — Classify and migrate thin evidence capabilities in vertical batches

Files per batch are disjoint by proposed check owner: implementation + rule + config/defaults + tests + docs. Shared DI/qmx files are a final serial integration package, not concurrently edited.

1. Refresh P0's exhaustive ownership table and add a 6–12 month focused co-change matrix excluding mass commits.
2. Keep the reviewed dispositions unless new evidence triggers a plan/ADR amendment: Halstead moves with Maintainability, WMC with Complexity, and legacy Structure declarations split among Cohesion, Size and Design exactly as enumerated in the generated inventory.
3. For each accepted capability, update its P7 manifest rows and named consumers, regenerate qmx/inventories, then move metric collectors and rules together under `Analysis\Evidence\{Capability}`. Measurement foundation and repository work already closed in P3 are inputs, not P7 leftovers. Give a capability `Contract` only if another module actually consumes it.
4. Move the transitional `frameworkNamespaces` field to the Coupling-owned configuration contract; P7 may not extend the mixed P3 DTO.
5. Delete empty role buckets and shrink Metrics/Rules foundation; do not recreate identical internal role folders by template.

DoD per batch: one owner for every moved class; no cross-check internal import; external imports only through actual contracts; all rule names/options/output stay stable unless a documented breaking change is intentional; README gives an agent a bounded reading set.

```text
[complete] P0-A -> P0-B -> P0-C -> P0-D -> P0 completion gate
[complete] P1-A -> P1-B -> {P1-C || P1-D} -> P1-E -> P1 completion gate -> [complete] P2-A -> P2-B -> P2-D1 -> P2-C -> P2-D2 -> P2-E -> P2 completion gate -> [complete; independent design review GO] P3-0 -> [complete] P3-A -> [complete] P3-B -> [complete] P3-R1 -> [complete] P3-R2 -> independent remediation review -> [complete] P3-R3 -> independent P3-R3 review -> [checkpoint complete] P3-C -> final-review findings -> [complete] {P3-R4-A || P3-R4-B} -> [complete] P3-R4-C -> P3-R4-D -> P3-R4-E -> aggregate validation -> [complete; GO] final independent re-review -> [next/pending] P4 -> P5 -> P6 -> P7 -> P8
```

P0 completed discovery, decision and enforcement; it does not authorise the unchanged remainder automatically. P0-A atomically established authority, generated qmx enforcement, reproducible evidence and freshness checks, including ownership of the qmx topology header immediately adjacent to its generated region. P0-B closed decision/general documentation, P0-C reconciled the baseline, and P0-D completed final review and shared-document dispositions. P1, P2 and P3 are complete. P3-A, P3-B, P3-R1, P3-R2, P3-R3 and P3-R4 are complete, P3-C has published the final byte-stable result, aggregate validation is green, and final independent P3 re-review is GO with no findings. P4 is next/pending. Generated rows bearing an exact P1–P8 closure remain migration inputs; P3's exhaustive amendment tables reclassify them by subject and name every integration writer. Documentation rows marked `P0-D`, `permanent`, or `shared` do not grant later package ownership and still change when the landed current state would otherwise make them false. The only P3 Run-owned new port is FileSetInspection; DependencyTraversal is a DependencyModel-owned promise to its named consumers. No graph-preparation, metric-derivation or generic lifecycle port is approved.

Packages P1–P6 then land sequentially because they share Run, DI, configuration and topology seams. P1's temporary `MetricEnricher -> DuplicationInspectionInterface` relation created the explicit lifecycle dependency on P3; P3-A replaced it with FileSetInspection and deleted the temporary contract. P3-A migrated the original production/import/test/governance set and P3-B completed its documentation set. P3-R1 atomically owns the exact remediation source/tests plus finite manifest/generator/governance-source delta; P3-R2 owns only four named READMEs. P3-C owns only generated publication, qmx, baseline, PHPUnit discovery and the final status marker. P4 must prove any Architecture/CircularDependency boundary from its own inputs and closes the two physically renamed Configuration seams plus the transitional lifecycle/cycle result. P5 dissolves transitional enrichment and Health/computed fields; P6 closes the eight Finding deferrals and rule-option transport; P7 closes Coupling's framework namespace field. P7 batches may be delegated in parallel only after an execution plan enumerates exact non-overlapping production/tests/docs files; shared DI, `qmx.yaml`, `phpunit.xml.dist`, Composer dev-autoload/classmaps, root docs and generated inventories form a named serial integration package. No package described with `**` is eligible for parallel execution as-is.

Each package is behavioural-preservation first: move + contract + architecture tests in one package, no compatibility shim. Validate focused tests after every package, then `composer check`; run standard/extended review according to changed contracts and inspect seams explicitly.

## Edge cases and regression matrix

- two analyses with different config in one process;
- a disabled expensive capability does zero preparation/allocation;
- empty project and incomplete analysis result;
- parallel collection serialization contains only neutral contracts, never module services/state;
- config warnings emitted after logger setup without a global feature holder;
- deterministic extension order and duplicate-id failure;
- CLI debug/export adapters consume only public contracts;
- baseline + source suppression + CLI exclusion + git projection preserve exact order;
- Git projection changes only the reported view and never the accepted-state/policy result;
- taxonomy directories contain no production types and cannot be dependency targets;
- a listed contract consumer without an observed exact target import fails; a temporary consumer cannot omit/mismatch `source_fqcn`, cannot disagree with its semantic `owner`, and cannot omit or outlive `closes_in`;
- a fourth declaration of an otherwise authorised temporary consumer owner cannot reuse its coarse qmx allow;
- an unlisted exact internal import fails even when one of the 16 coarse semantic-owner edges would otherwise permit it;
- every moved test remains present in the PHPUnit discovery manifest, with no duplicate old/new class;
- module-specific fixtures and support code move with their owner while neutral TestSupport consumers remain green;
- layer policy and circular-dependency evidence can be enabled, disabled and reset independently;
- dynamic rule discovery and constructor dependencies still work after moves;
- cache payload versioning is bumped or proven compatible for moved class names;
- public contract break produces CHANGELOG migration entry;
- taxonomy container cannot become qmx allow target.

## Explicit non-goals

- no generic marketplace/plugin API for third-party checks;
- no `Api/`/`Contract/` folder for private leaf code;
- no `FindingEvaluation` role bucket or policy/projection state shared through the Run kernel;
- no placement of circular-dependency detection under Architecture merely because its rule currently lives there;
- no numeric context-window hard gate;
- no mass migration based only on names or current directory categories;
- no preservation shim for obsolete internal namespaces.
